Codebase intelligence</h3><p class="font-body text-[1rem] font-normal leading-[1.35rem] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">Codegraph and custom guidelines help us understand complex dependencies across files to uncover the impact of changes.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col flex-1 p-7.5 rounded-[0.1875rem] bg-gray-900"><div class="w-[80%] h-28 md:h-24 sm:h-36 mb-4 md:mb-0 lg:mb-7.5 relative"><img alt="" title="" loading="lazy" width="305" height="99" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="object-center dark:hidden object-contain" style="color:transparent" src="/content/assets/external-context.svg?dpl=dpl_2qZDNRCtYrvJqdyTUbuRRgb1L3tR"/><img alt="" title="" loading="lazy" width="305" height="99" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="object-center hidden dark:block object-contain" style="color:transparent" src="/content/assets/external-context.svg?dpl=dpl_2qZDNRCtYrvJqdyTUbuRRgb1L3tR"/></div><div><h3 class="flex flex-col justify-center font-medium text-heading-xs mb-4">2. External context</h3><p class="font-body text-[1rem] font-normal leading-[1.35rem] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">We bring the right context via MCP servers, Linked Issues (Jira &amp; Linear) &amp; Web Query (to fetch the latest info on the web). </p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col flex-1 p-7.5 rounded-[0.1875rem] bg-gray-900"><div class="w-[80%] h-28 md:h-24 sm:h-36 mb-4 md:mb-0 lg:mb-7.5 relative"><img alt="" title="" loading="lazy" width="306" height="115" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="object-center dark:hidden object-contain" style="color:transparent" src="/content/assets/scan-linter.svg?dpl=dpl_2qZDNRCtYrvJqdyTUbuRRgb1L3tR"/><img alt="" title="" loading="lazy" width="306" height="115" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="object-center hidden dark:block object-contain" style="color:transparent" src="/content/assets/scan-linter.svg?dpl=dpl_2qZDNRCtYrvJqdyTUbuRRgb1L3tR"/></div><div><h3 class="flex flex-col justify-center font-medium text-heading-xs mb-4">3. Linters &amp; Scanners</h3><p class="font-body text-[1rem] font-normal leading-[1.35rem] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">40+ linters and security scanners catch more bugs – while we filter out the noise from false positives.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="container mx-auto w-full px-4 md:px-6 lg:px-12 xl:max-w-[120rem] xl:px-30" id=""><div class="mb-8 gap-4 flex flex-col"><div class="justify-between flex"><div class="inline-flex items-center font-mono text-eyebrow max-[23.0625rem]:text-[0.625rem] text-orange-500 font-normal pr-2 py-1 "><span class="inline-flex" aria-label="CR_Intelligence" style="opacity:1"><span class="inline-block bg-orange-500/15
